- **sprint-loop**: /loop 5m What are you working on? What's left to do? Anything you need help with unblocking? Make sure that all blockers/deferred items are created as tracker items. keep running /cycle until the tracker backlog is empty (sprints.backlog == 0 and items.backlog == 0). If the backlog is empty, have the entire agent panel perform a full codebase review. ([ventifus/tinct](https://github.com/ventifus/tinct/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sprint-loop.md))
- **sprint-loopback**: Loopback the Sprint. Concept §7 — make a "no" cheap. ([cgbarlow/mitchell-agentic-sprint](https://github.com/cgbarlow/mitchell-agentic-sprint/blob/HEAD/commands/sprint-loopback.md) · ★1)
